企業簡介

China Lesso Group Holdings Limited is a leading large-scale             中國聯塑集團控股有限公司是中國內地領先的大
industrial group that manufactures building materials and interior      型建材家居產業集團，於香港聯合交易所有限公
decoration products in mainland China. It is listed on the main board   司主板上市（股票簡稱：中國聯塑，股份代號：
of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (stock name: China           2128）。中國聯塑是恒生綜合指數成份股中型股
Lesso, stock code: 2128). China Lesso is one of the constituent         之一，並被獲納入為港股通股份。本集團提供過
stocks of the Hang Seng Composite Index – MidCap and is covered         萬種優質產品，涵蓋塑料管道系統、水暖衛浴產
by Hong Kong Stock Exchange’s Stock Connect. The Group                  品、整體廚房、門窗系統、裝飾板材、消防器材
provides over 10,000 types of quality products, including plastic       及衛生材料等類別，並廣泛應用於家居裝修、給
pipes piping system, sanitary ware products, integrated kitchens,       水、排水、電力供應及通訊、燃氣輸送、農用、
systems of doors and windows, decorative plates, fire-fighting          水產養殖業、地暖和消防等領域，為中國建材家
equipment, and sanitary materials, etc. They are widely applied to      居領域產品體系最為齊全的生產商之一。
fields such as interior decoration, water supply, drainage, power
supply and telecommunications, gas transmission, agriculture,
aquaculture, floor heating and fire services. The Group is positioned
as one of the manufacturers who offer the most comprehensive
range of building materials and interior decoration products in
China.

Shandong and Hunan. The Group has established a nationwide              進生產基地。本集團擁有覆蓋全國的分銷網絡，
sales network and has also developed long-term strategic                與 2,193 名的獨立獨家一級經銷商建立長期戰略

     conditions may cause the Group to cease to continue as a

     Public Accountants, accounting principles generally accepted               製。本 綜 合 財 務 報 表 按 歷 史 成 本 法 編 製，

     Kong Companies Ordinance. They have been prepared                          資產及衍生金融工具除外。本綜合財務報
     under the historical cost convention, except for investment                表 乃 以 人 民 幣 呈 列，除 另 有 指 明 外，所 有

     instruments which have been measured at fair value. These

     A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly        附屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制的實體
     or indirectly controlled by the Company. Control is achieved               （包括結構性實體）。當本集團承受或享有
     when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns              參與投資對象業務所得的可變回報，且能
     from its involvement with the investee and has the ability to              透過對投資對象的權力（即賦予本集團現有
     affect those returns through its power over the investee (i.e.,            能力主導投資對象相關活動的既存權利）影
     existing rights that give the Group the current ability to direct          響該等回報時，即取得控制權。
     the relevant activities of the investee).

         time.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                     2.4 主要會計政策概要
    POLICIES

      INVESTMENTS IN ASSOCIATES AND JOINT VENTURES                          於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
      An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term          聯營公司為本集團擁有一般不少於 20% 股
      interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting          本投票權的長期權益的實體，並可對其發
      rights and over which it is in a position to exercise significant     揮重大影響力。重大影響力指參與投資對
      influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in       象的財政及經營決策之權力，而非控制或
      the financial and operating policy decisions of the investee,         共同控制該等決策之權力。
      but is not control or joint control over those policies.

      A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the            合 營 企 業 為 一 項 合 營 安 排，據 此，擁 有 安
      parties that have joint control of the arrangement have rights        排之共同控制權的人士均有權享有合營企
      to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint control is the          業的淨資產。共同控制乃以合約協定分佔
      contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,            一項安排之控制權，其僅在相關活動決策
      which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities        必須獲分佔控制權的人士一致同意時方存
      require unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.             在。

      The Group’s investments in associates and joint ventures are          本集團於聯營公司及合營企業的投資乃按
      stated in the consolidated statement of financial position at         權益會計法核算，按本集團應佔淨資產扣
      the Group’s share of net assets under the equity method of            除任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況表中呈列。
      accounting, less any impairment losses.

      The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other           本集團應佔聯營公司及合營企業收購後業
      comprehensive income of associates and joint ventures                 績和其他全面收益分別計入損益及其他全
      is included in profit or loss and other comprehensive                 面 收 益 內。此 外，倘 直 接 於 聯 營 公 司 或 合
      income, respectively. In addition, when there has been a              營企業的權益確認變動，則本集團會於綜
      change recognised directly in the equity of the associates            合 權 益 變 動 表 確 認 其 應 佔 的 任 何 變 動（如
      or joint ventures, the Group recognises its share of any              適用）。本集團與其聯營公司或合營企業進
      changes, when applicable, in the consolidated statement               行交易而出現的未實現利益及虧損會按本
      of changes in equity. Unrealised gains and losses resulting           集團於聯營公司或合營企業的權益互相抵
      from transactions between the Group and its associates or             銷，但如果未實現虧損證明所轉讓資產發
      joint ventures are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s            生減值則除外。收購聯營公司或合營企業
      interests in the associates or joint ventures, except where           產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公司或合營
      unrealised losses provide evidence of an impairment of the            企業的投資的一部分。
      asset transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition of
      associates or joint ventures is included as part of the Group’s
      investments in associates or joint ventures.

   INTEREST IN JOINT OPERATION                                               於合營業務之權益
   A joint operation is a joint arrangement whereby the parties              合 營 業 務 為 共 同 安 排，據 此，擁 有 該 安 排
   that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to                 共同控制權的各方有權享有與該安排有關
   the assets, and obligations for the liabilities, relating to              的資產及就負債承擔責任。共同控制乃以
   the arrangement. Joint control is the contractually agreed                合約協定分佔一項安排之控制權，其僅在
   sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only when              相關活動決策必須獲分佔控制權的人士一
   decisions about the relevant activities require the unanimous             致同意時方存在。
   consent of the parties sharing control.

   The Group recognises in relation to its interest in a joint               本集團就其於合營業務之權益確認：
   operation:

   •    its assets, including its share of any assets held jointly;          •   其資產，包括分佔任何共同持有之資
                                                                                 產；

   •    its liabilities, including its share of any liabilities incurred     •   其負債，包括分佔任何共同產生之負
        jointly;                                                                 債；

   •    its revenue from the sale of its share of the output                 •   出售其於合營業務應佔產出之所得收
        arising from the joint operation;                                        入；

   •    its share of the revenue from the sale of the output by              •   出售合營業務產出之應佔收入；及
        the joint operation; and

   •    its expenses, including its share of any expenses                    •   其開支，包括分佔任何共同產生之開
        incurred jointly.                                                        支。

   The assets, liabilities, revenues and expenses relating to                本集團於合營業務之權益之有關資產、負
   the Group’s interest in a joint operation are accounted for               債、收 入 及 開 支 乃 根 據 適 用 於 相 關 資 產、
   in accordance with the HKFRSs applicable to the particular                負債、收入及開支之香港財務報告準則入
   assets, liabilities, revenues and expenses.                               賬。

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                    2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    POLICIES (Continued)

      BUSINESS COMBINATIONS AND GOODWILL                                   業務合併及商譽
      Business combinations are accounted for using the                    業務合併乃以購買法入賬。轉讓之代價乃
      acquisition method. The consideration transferred is                 以收購日期的公允價值計算，該公允價值
      measured at the acquisition-date fair value which is the sum         為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公允價
      of the acquisition-date fair values of assets transferred by         值、本集團自被收購方之前度擁有人承擔
      the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group to the former            的負債，及本集團發行以換取被收購方控
      owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued               制權的股本權益的總和。於各業務合併中，
      by the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree. For            本集團選擇是否以公允價值或被收購方可
      each business combination, the Group elects whether to               識別資產淨值的應佔比例，計算於被收購
      measure the non-controlling interests in the acquiree that           方屬現時擁有權權益並賦予其持有人有權
      are present ownership interests and entitle their holders to a       於清盤時按比例分佔實體的淨資產的非控
      proportionate share of net assets in the event of liquidation        制權益。非控制權益的一切其他成分乃按
      either at fair value or at the proportionate share of the            公允價值計量。收購相關成本於產生時列
      acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components             為開支。
      of non-controlling interests are measured at fair value.
      Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.

      When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial        當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合約條
      assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification        款、於 收 購 日 期 的 經 濟 環 境 及 相 關 條 件，
      and designation in accordance with the contractual terms,            評估將承接的金融資產及負債，以作出適
      economic circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the            當的分類及標示，其中包括分離被收購方
      acquisition date. This includes the separation of embedded           主合約中的嵌入式衍生工具。
      derivatives in host contracts of the acquiree.

      If the business combination is achieved in stages, the               倘業務合併分階段進行，以往持有的股本
      previously held equity interest is remeasured at its                 權益會以收購日期的公允價值重新計量，
      acquisition-date fair value and any resulting gain or loss is        任何因此帶來的利益或虧損於損益中確認。
      recognised in profit or loss.

      Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer       將由收購方轉讓的任何或然代價於收購日
      is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent      期按公允價值確認。或然代價按公允價值
      consideration classified as an asset or liability is measured        計量分類為資產或負債，而公允價值變動
      at fair value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or     則於損益確認。或然代價並不會重新計量，
      loss. Contingent consideration that is classified as equity is       其後結算於權益中入賬。
      not remeasured and subsequent settlement is accounted for
      within equity.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                 2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    POLICIES (Continued)

   BUSINESS COMBINATIONS AND GOODWILL (Continued)                       業務合併及商譽（續）
   Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess of          商 譽 初 始 按 成 本 計 量，即 已 轉 讓 代 價、已
   the aggregate of the consideration transferred, the amount           確認非控制權益金額及本集團先前持有被
   recognised for non-controlling interests and any fair value of       收購方股本權益的任何公允價值之總額超
   the Group’s previously held equity interests in the acquiree         出所收購可識別資產淨值及所承擔負債的
   over the identifiable net assets acquired and liabilities            差額。倘此代價及其他項目的總和低於所
   assumed. If the sum of this consideration and other items            收購淨資產的公允價值，則再評估後的差
   is lower than the fair value of the net assets acquired, the         額於損益確認為議價收購所得利益。
   difference is, after reassessment, recognised in profit or loss
   as a gain on bargain purchase.

   After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less         於初始確認後，商譽按成本減任何累計減
   any accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for            值 虧 損 計 量。商 譽 須 每 年 進 行 減 值 測 試，
   impairment annually or more frequently if events or changes          若有事件發生或情況改變顯示賬面值可能
   in circumstances indicate that the carrying value may be             減 值，則 會 更 頻 密 地 進 行 測 試。本 集 團 將
   impaired. The Group will perform its annual impairment test          於 12 月 31 日進行商譽的年度減值測試。為
   of goodwill as at 31 December. For the purpose of impairment         進行減值測試，無論本集團其他資產或負
   testing, goodwill acquired in a business combination is, from        債是否已分配至現金產生單位或現金產生
   the acquisition date, allocated to each of the Group’s cash-         單位組別，因業務合併而購入的商譽自收
   generating units, or groups of cash-generating units, that are       購日期起分配至預期可從合併產生的協同
   expected to benefit from the synergies of the combination,           效益中獲益的本集團各現金產生單位或現
   irrespective of whether other assets or liabilities of the Group     金產生單位組別。
   are assigned to those units or groups of units.

   Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable                減值乃通過評估與商譽有關的現金產生單
   amount of the cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating         位（現 金 產 生 單 位 組 別）的 可 收 回 金 額 釐
   units) to which the goodwill relates. Where the recoverable          定。當現金產生單位（現金產生單位組別）
   amount of the cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating         的可收回金額低於賬面值時，則確認減值
   units) is less than the carrying amount, an impairment loss is       虧損。已確認商譽減值虧損不得於其後期
   recognised. An impairment loss recognised for goodwill is not        間撥回。
   reversed in a subsequent period.

   Where goodwill has been allocated to a cash-generating unit          倘商譽已分配至現金產生單位（或現金產生
   (or group of cash-generating units) and part of the operation        單位組別）而該單位的部分業務已出售，則
   within that unit is disposed of, the goodwill associated with        與所出售業務相關的商譽會在釐定出售的
   the operation disposed of is included in the carrying amount         利益或虧損時計入該業務的賬面值。在該
   of the operation when determining the gain or loss on the            等情況下出售的商譽，乃根據所出售業務
   disposal. Goodwill disposed of in these circumstances                的相對價值及現金產生單位的保留份額計
   is measured based on the relative value of the operation             量。
   disposed of and the portion of the cash-generating unit

      FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT                                                  公允價值之計量
      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an               公允價值為市場參與者於計量日期在有序
      asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction         交易中出售資產所收取的價格或轉讓負債
      between market participants at the measurement date. The                所支付的價格。公允價值計量乃假設出售
      fair value measurement is based on the presumption that the             資產或轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要
      transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place     市場或（在無主要市場情況下）最具優勢市
      either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in        場進行而作出。主要或最具優勢市場須為
      the absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous             本集團可進入之市場。資產或負債的公允
      market for the asset or liability. The principal or the most            價值乃基於市場參與者於資產或負債定價
      advantageous market must be accessible by the Group.                    所用的假設計量（即假設市場參與者會以最
      The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using the         佳經濟利益行事）。
      assumptions that market participants would use when pricing
      the asset or liability, assuming that market participants act in
      their economic best interest.

      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into            非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及市場參
      account a market participant’s ability to generate economic             與者透過最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用
      benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by           途，或將該資產出售予將最大限度使用該
      selling it to another market participant that would use the             資產達致最佳用途的其他市場參與者以產
      asset in its highest and best use.                                      生經濟利益的能力。

      The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in             本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備充分數
      the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available           據以供計量公允價值的估值方法，以盡量
      to measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant                   使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少使用
      observable inputs and minimising the use of unobservable                不可觀察輸入數據。
      inputs.

      All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured             所有於本綜合財務報表計量或披露公允價
      or disclosed in the consolidated financial statements are               值的資產及負債乃基於對公允價值計量整
      categorised within the fair value hierarchy, described as               體而言屬重大的最低層輸入數據按以下公
      follows, based on the lowest level input that is significant to         允價值層級分類：
      the fair value measurement as a whole:

      Level 1   –     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active           第一級   －   基於相同資產或負債於活躍
                      markets for identical assets or liabilities                       市場的報價（未經調整）

      Level 2   –     based on valuation techniques for which the             第二級   －   按估值方法計量，而對公允
                      lowest level input that is significant to the                     價值計量而言屬重大的最低
                      fair value measurement is observable, either                      層輸入數據可直接或間接觀
                      directly or indirectly                                            察得出

      Level 3   –     based on valuation techniques for which the             第三級   －   按估值方法計量，而對公允
                      lowest level input that is significant to the fair                價值計量而言屬重大的最低
                      value measurement is unobservable                                 層輸入數據不可觀察得出

   IMPAIRMENT OF NON-FINANCIAL ASSETS                                    非金融資產減值
   Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual              倘資產存在減值跡象或須進行年度減值測
   impairment testing for an asset is required (other than               試（存貨、工程合約資產、遞延稅項資產及
   inventories, construction contract asset, deferred tax assets         金融資產除外），則會就該項資產的可收回
   and financial assets), the asset’s recoverable amount is              金額作出估計。資產的可收回金額為資產
   estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount is the higher of the         或現金產生單位的可使用價值與其公允價
   asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value in use and its fair value     值減出售成本兩者中之較高者，並就個別
   less costs of disposal, and is determined for an individual           資產而釐定，除非該項資產並不產生在頗
   asset, unless the asset does not generate cash inflows                大程度上獨立於其他資產或資產組別的現
   that are largely independent of those from other assets or            金 流 入，在 此 情 況 下，可 收 回 金 額 以 該 項
   groups of assets, in which case the recoverable amount is             資產所屬的現金產生單位釐定。
   determined for the cash-generating unit to which the asset
   belongs.

   An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount          資產的賬面值超逾其可收回金額時則確認
   of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing              減值虧損。於評估可使用價值時，估計未來
   value in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted          現金流量將以除稅前貼現率折讓至現值，
   to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that             該貼現率反映現時市場對貨幣時間值及該
   reflects current market assessments of the time value of              項資產的特定風險的評估。減值虧損將於
   money and the risks specific to the asset. An impairment loss         其產生期間於損益內與減值資產的功能一
   is charged to profit or loss in the period in which it arises in      致的開支類別中扣除。
   those expense categories consistent with the function of the
   impaired asset.

   An assessment is made at the end of each reporting                    於各報告期末會評估是否有跡象顯示於過
   period as to whether there is an indication that previously           往確認的減值虧損可能不再存在或可能已
   recognised impairment losses may no longer exist or may               減 少。倘 存 在 有 關 跡 象，則 會 就 有 關 可 收
   have decreased. If such an indication exists, the recoverable         回金額作出估計。僅於用以釐定資產的可
   amount is estimated. A previously recognised impairment               收回金額的估計出現變動時，過往確認商
   loss of an asset other than goodwill is reversed only if there        譽以外的資產的減值虧損方可撥回，但撥
   has been a change in the estimates used to determine the              回金額不得高於假設過往年度並無就該資
   recoverable amount of that asset, but not to an amount higher         產確認減值虧損而應有之賬面值（扣除任何
   than the carrying amount that would have been determined              折舊╱攤銷）。該減值虧損的撥回於其產生
   (net of any depreciation/amortisation) had no impairment              期間計入損益內。
   loss been recognised for the asset in prior years. A reversal
   of such an impairment loss is credited to profit or loss in the
   period in which it arises.

   is capitalised and deferred only when the Group can                    能夠證明形成無形資產以致其將可供使用
   demonstrate the technical feasibility of completing the                或出售的技術可行性、其形成資產的意欲
   intangible asset so that it will be available for use or sale, its     及使用或出售資產的能力、該資產將如何
   intention to complete and its ability to use or sell the asset,        產生未來經濟利益、完成項目的資源可得
   how the asset will generate future economic benefits, the              性，以及可靠計量於開發期間的開支的能
   availability of resources to complete the project and the ability      力時方會被資本化及遞延。不符合該等標
   to measure reliably the expenditure during the development.            準的產品開發開支於產生時列賬為支出。
   Product development expenditure which does not meet these
   criteria is expensed when incurred.

   Deferred development costs are stated at cost less                     遞延開發成本按照與個別收購的無形資產
   accumulated amortisation and accumulated impairment                    相同的基準，按成本減累計攤銷及累計減
   losses (if any), on the same basis as intangible assets that are       值虧損（如有）列賬。
   acquired separately.

   Patents and Proprietary Technologies                                   專利及專有技術
   Purchased patents and proprietary technologies acquired are            已收購之專利及專有技術以成本減任何減
   stated at cost less any impairment losses and are amortised            值虧損列賬，並以直線法按其估計可使用
   on the straight-line basis over their estimated useful lives of 5      年期 5 年至 20 年攤銷。
   years to 20 years.

      LEASES                                                                租賃
      Finance Leases                                                        融資租賃
      Leases that transfer substantially all the rewards and risks of       凡將資產擁有權（法定業權除外）的大部分
      ownership of assets to the Group, other than legal title, are         回報與風險轉讓至本集團的租賃均列為融
      accounted for as finance leases. At the inception of a finance        資 租 賃。於 融 資 租 賃 生 效 時，租 賃 資 產 之
      lease, the cost of the leased asset is capitalised at the present     成本乃按最低租金現值撥充資本，並連同
      value of the minimum lease payments and recorded together             債務（不包括利息部分）一併入賬，以反映
      with the obligation, excluding the interest element, to reflect       購買及融資狀況。根據資本化融資租賃持
      the purchase and financing. Assets held under capitalised             有 的 資 產（包 括 融 資 租 賃 之 預 付 土 地 租 賃
      finance leases, including prepaid land lease payments under           款）乃計入物業、廠房及設備，並按租期與
      finance leases, are included in property, plant and equipment,        資產的估計可用年期兩者中的較短者計算
      and depreciated over the shorter of the lease terms and the           折舊。該等租賃的融資成本乃在損益內扣
      estimated useful lives of the assets. The finance costs of such       除，以於租期內作出定期定額扣減。
      leases are charged to profit or loss so as to provide a constant
      periodic rate of charge over the lease terms.

      Assets acquired through hire purchase contracts of a                  透過融資性質的租購合約收購的資產乃列
      financing nature are accounted for as finance leases, but are         為融資租賃，並於其估計可用年期內折舊。
      depreciated over their estimated useful lives.

      measures a financial asset at its fair value plus, in the case of     入 損 益 的 金 融 資 產 則 另 加 交 易 成 本）計 量
      a financial asset not at FVTPL, transaction costs. Trade and          金融資產。並無包含重大融資成分或本集

      The Group’s business model for managing financial assets              本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其如何
      refers to how it manages its financial assets in order to             管理金融資產以產生現金流量。業務模式
      generate cash flows. The business model determines                    釐定現金流量會否產生自收取合約現金流
      whether cash flows will result from collecting contractual            量、出售金融資產，或兩者兼有。
      cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both.

      All regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are          所有以日常買賣之金融資產，均按交易日

      delivery of assets within the period generally established by

   FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (POLICIES UNDER HKAS 39                             金融工具（於 2018 年 1 月 1 日前適用的香港
   APPLICABLE BEFORE 1 JANUARY 2018)                                         會計準則第 39 號政策）
   Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a          當一集團實體成為工具合約條款之一方時，
   group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of             金融資產及金融負債予以確認。
   the instruments.

   Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured         金融資產及金融負債按公允價值作初始計
   at fair value. Transaction costs that are directly attributable           量。收購或發行金融資產及金融負債（按公
   to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and financial             允價值計入損益之金融資產及金融負債除
   liabilities (other than financial assets and financial liabilities        外）之直接應佔交易成本於初始確認時計入
   at FVTPL) are added to or deducted from the fair value of the             或扣自各金融資產或金融負債（如適用）之
   financial assets or financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial     公允價值。收購按公允價值計入損益之金
   recognition. Transaction costs directly attributable to the               融資產或金融負債之直接應佔交易成本即
   acquisition of financial assets or financial liabilities at FVTPL         時於損益中確認。金融資產及金融負債之
   are recognised immediately in profit or loss. The subsequent              其後計量視乎其分類而定。
   measurement of financial assets and financial liabilities
   depends on their classification.

      FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (POLICIES UNDER HKAS 39                        金融工具（於 2018 年 1 月 1 日前適用的香港

      Effective interest method                                            實際利率法
      The effective interest method is a method of calculating the         實際利率法乃一種計算債務工具或金融負
      amortised cost of a debt instrument or financial liability and       債之攤銷成本及在相關期間內分配利息收
      of allocating interest income or expense over the relevant           益或開支的方法。實際利率乃按債務工具
      period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly         或金融負債之預計年期或（倘適用）較短期
      discounts estimated future cash receipts or payments                 間，準確貼現估計未來現金收入或付款（包
      (including all fees and points paid or received that form an         括構成實際利率不可或缺部分之一切已付
      integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs      或已收利率差價費用、交易成本及其他溢價
      and other premiums or discounts) through the expected                或折讓）至初始確認時之賬面淨值之利率。
      life of the debt instrument or financial liability, or where
      appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on
      initial recognition.

      Interest income or expense is recognised on an effective             利息收益或開支乃按實際利率法確認。
      interest method.

      Financial Assets                                                     金融資產
      Financial assets are classified into the following specified         金融資產歸類為以下具體類別，按公允價
      categories: financial assets at FVTPL, held-to-maturity              值計入損益之金融資產、持有至到期投資、
      investments, loans and receivables and available-for-sale            貸款及應收款項及可供出售金融資產。於
      financial assets. The classification depends on the nature and       初始確認時，視乎金融資產之性質及其目
      purpose of the financial assets and is determined at the time        的 而 分 類。所 有 以 日 常 買 賣 之 金 融 資 產，
      of initial recognition. All regular way purchases or sales of        均按交易日基準進行確認及終止確認。日
      financial assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade          常買賣乃指須於市場規定或慣例一般所訂
      date basis. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or          的時間內交付資產的金融資產買賣。
      sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets within
      the time frame established by regulation or convention in the
      marketplace.

      designated as at FVTPL upon initial recognition.                     計入損益之金融資產。

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                      2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）

      IMPAIRMENT OF FINANCIAL ASSETS (POLICIES UNDER                         金融資產減值（自 2018 年 1 月 1 日起適用的
      HKFRS 9 APPLICABLE FROM 1 JANUARY 2018)                                香港財務報告準則第 9 號政策）
      The Group recognises an allowance for ECLs for all debt                本集團就並非按公允價值計入損益的所有
      instruments not held at FVTPL. ECLs are based on the                   債務工具確認預期信貸虧損撥備。預期信
      difference between the contractual cash flows due in                   貸虧損乃基於根據合約應付的合約現金流
      accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that the           量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間
      Group expects to receive, discounted at an approximation of            的 差 額，並 按 接 近 原 有 實 際 利 率 貼 現。預
      the original effective interest rate. The expected cash flows          期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押品或合約
      will include cash flows from the sale of collateral held or            條款所包含的其他信貸增強措施所得的現
      other credit enhancements that are integral to the contractual         金流量。
      terms.

      General Approach                                                       一般方法
      ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for            預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初始
      which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk         確認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸風
      since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses         險而言，會為未來 12 個月內的可能違約事
      that result from default events that are possible within the           件所產生的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧損撥
      next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures            備（12 個月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始確認
      for which there has been a significant increase in credit risk         以來信貸風險大幅增加的信貸風險而言，
      since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit     不論違約時間如何，須就預期於風險餘下
      losses expected over the remaining life of the exposure,               存續期內的信貸虧損計提虧損撥備（整個存
      irrespective of the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).            續期預期信貸虧損）。

      At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the                 於各報告日期，本集團評估自初始確認後
      credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly      金融工具的信貸風險是否大幅增加。於進
      since initial recognition. When making the assessment,                 行評估時，本集團將於報告日期金融工具
      the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the              發生違約的風險與初始確認日期金融工具
      financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a       發生違約的風險進行比較，並考慮所有毋
      default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of        須付出不必要成本或努力即可獲得的合理
      initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable           支持資料，包括歷史及前瞻性資料。
      information that is available without undue cost or effort,
      including historical and forward-looking information.

      The Group considers a financial asset in default when                  當合約付款已逾期 5 年，本集團認為金融資
      contractual payments are 5 years past due. However, in                 產 將 會 違 約。可 是，於 某 些 情 況 下，倘 內
      certain cases, the Group may also consider a financial                 部或外部資料顯示在並無計及本集團任何
      asset to be in default when internal or external information           信貸增強措施前，本集團不大可能悉數收
      indicates that the Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding        到未償還合約款項，本集團亦可能認為金
      contractual amounts in full before taking into account any             融資產將會違約。於無法合理預期收回合
      credit enhancements held by the Group. A financial asset               約現金流量時撇銷金融資產。
      is written off when there is no reasonable expectation of
      recovering the contractual cash flows.

             reporting date (but that are not purchased or                  購買或原先信貸減值）且其虧損
             originated credit-impaired) and for which the loss             撥備的金額乃按相等於整個存

   Simplified Approach                                                 簡化方法
   For trade and bills receivables and contract assets that do not     就並無包含重大融資成分或本集團應用可
   contain a significant financing component or when the Group         行權宜方法不調整重大融資成分影響的貿
   applies the practical expedient of not adjusting the effect         易應收款項及票據和合約資產而言，本集

   simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the simplified       簡化方法，本集團並無追蹤信貸風險的變

   matrix that is based on its historical credit loss experience,      務人及經濟環境的特定前瞻性因素作出調

   and the economic environment.

      IMPAIRMENT OF FINANCIAL ASSETS (POLICIES UNDER                        金融資產減值（於 2018 年 1 月 1 日前適用的
      HKAS 39 APPLICABLE BEFORE 1 JANUARY 2018)                             香港會計準則第 39 號政策）
      The Group assesses at the end of each reporting period                本集團於各報告期末評估有否客觀迹象顯
      whether there is objective evidence that a financial asset            示一項或一組金融資產出現減值。倘於初
      or a group of financial assets is impaired. An impairment             始確認資產後發生的一項或多項事件對該
      exists if one or more events that occurred after the initial          項或該組金融資產的估計未來現金流量造
      recognition of the asset have an impact on the estimated              成影響，而該影響能夠可靠地估計，則該項
      future cash flows of the financial asset or the group of              或該組金融資產出現減值。減值跡象可能
      financial assets that can be reliably estimated. Evidence of          包括一名債務人或一組債務人正在經歷重
      impairment may include indications that a debtor or a group           大 財 務 困 難、拖 欠 利 息 或 本 金、可 能 破 產
      of debtors is experiencing significant financial difficulty,          或進行其他財務重組，以及有明顯數據顯
      default or delinquency in interest or principal payments, the         示估計未來現金流量出現可計量的減少，
      probability that they will enter bankruptcy or other financial        例如欠款數目變動或與違約相關的經濟狀
      reorganisation and observable data indicating that there is a         況。
      measurable decrease in the estimated future cash flows, such
      as changes in arrears or economic conditions that correlate
      with defaults.

      Financial Assets Carried at Amortised Cost                            按攤銷成本列賬的金融資產
      For financial assets carried at amortised cost, the Group first       有關按攤銷成本列賬的金融資產，本集團
      assesses whether impairment exists individually for financial         首先獨立評估單項重大的金融資產有否減
      assets that are individually significant, or collectively for         值跡象，或共同評估非單項重大的金融資
      financial assets that are not individually significant. If the        產有否客觀減值跡象。倘本集團釐定個別
      Group determines that no objective evidence of impairment             已評估金融資產為並無客觀減值跡象，則
      exists for an individually assessed financial asset, whether          有關資產不論是否重大，會計入一組有相
      significant or not, it includes the asset in a group of financial     似信貸風險特徵的金融資產，共同作減值
      assets with similar credit risk characteristics and collectively      評 估。對 於 個 別 作 減 值 評 估 的 資 產，倘 其
      assesses them for impairment. Assets that are individually            確認或持續確認減值虧損，則不會計入共
      assessed for impairment and for which an impairment loss is,          同減值評估。
      or continues to be, recognised are not included in a collective
      assessment of impairment.

      The amount of any impairment loss identified is measured as           任何已識別的減值虧損按資產賬面值與估
      the difference between the asset’s carrying amount and the            計未來現金流量（不包括尚未產生的未來信
      present value of estimated future cash flows (excluding future        貸虧損）的現值之差額計量。估計未來現金
      credit losses that have not yet been incurred). The present           流量的現值會按金融資產的原實際利率折
      value of the estimated future cash flows is discounted at the         讓。
      financial asset’s original effective interest rate.

      Available-For-Sale Financial Assets                                   可供出售金融資產
      For available-for-sale financial assets, the Group assesses at        對於可供出售金融資產而言，本集團於各
      the end of each reporting period whether there is objective           報告期末評估是否有客觀證據顯示一項金
      evidence that a financial asset or a group of financial assets is     融資產或一組金融資產出現減值。
      impaired.

      If an available-for-sale financial asset is impaired, an amount       倘一項可供出售金融資產出現減值，其成
      comprising the difference between its cost (net of any                本值（扣除任何本金付款及攤銷）與其現時
      principal payment and amortisation) and its current fair value,       公允價值之差額減先前於損益內確認之任
      less any impairment loss previously recognised in profit or           何減值虧損之金額，會從其他全面收益中
      loss, is removed from other comprehensive income and                  剔除，並於損益內確認。
      recognised in profit or loss.

      In the case of equity investments classified as available-            倘屬分類為可供出售股權投資，則客觀證
      for-sale, objective evidence would include a significant or           據包括一項投資之公允價值大幅度或長期
      prolonged decline in the fair value of an investment below            降至低於其成本值。「大幅度」是相對於初
      its cost. “Significant” is evaluated against the original cost of     始投資成本作評估，而「長期」則是評估公
      the investment and “prolonged” against the period in which            允價值低於初始成本之期間。倘出現減值
      the fair value has been below its original cost. Where there is       證據，累計虧損（按收購成本與當前公允價
      evidence of impairment, the cumulative loss – measured as             值之差額減該項投資先前於損益內確認之
      the difference between the acquisition cost and the current           任何減值虧損計量）將從其他全面收益中剔
      fair value, less any impairment loss on that investment               除，並 於 損 益 內 確 認。分 類 為 可 供 出 售 之
      previously recognised in profit or loss – is removed from             股本工具之減值虧損不可透過損益撥回，
      other comprehensive income and recognised in profit or                而其減值後增加的公允價值會直接於其他
      loss. Impairment losses on equity instruments classified              全面收益中確認。
      as available-for-sale are not reversed through profit or loss.
      Increases in their fair value after impairment are recognised
      directly in other comprehensive income.

      The determination of what is “significant” or “prolonged”             釐定「大幅度」或「長期」時需要作出判斷。
      requires judgement. In making this judgement, the Group               在作出此項判斷時，本集團評估（其中包括）
      evaluates, among other factors, the duration or extent to             某項投資的公允價值低於其成本值的持續
      which the fair value of an investment is less than its cost.          時間或數額等因素。

   CONSTRUCTION CONTRACTS (APPLICABLE BEFORE                           工程合約（於 2018 年 1 月 1 日前適用）
   1 JANUARY 2018)
   Contract revenue comprises the agreed contract amount               合約收入包括協定合約金額及來自修訂合
   and appropriate amounts from variation orders, claims and           約、申 索 及 獎 金 之 適 當 金 額。已 產 生 合 約
   incentive payments. Contract costs incurred comprise direct         成 本 包 括 直 接 物 料、分 包 成 本、直 接 勞 工
   materials, the costs of subcontracting, direct labour and an        以及變動及固定工程間接成本的應佔部分。
   appropriate proportion of variable and fixed construction
   overheads.

   When the outcome of a construction contract can be                  倘工程合約的結果能可靠地計量，來自固
   estimated reliably, contract revenue from a fixed price             定價格工程合約的合約收入使用完成百分
   construction contract is recognised using the percentage of         比方式確認，乃經參考工程迄今已產生合
   completion method, measured by reference to the proportion          約成本對有關合約估計總成本的比例後計
   of contract costs incurred for work performed to date relative      量，惟 此 並 不 代 表 工 程 完 成 階 段 時 除 外。
   to the estimated total cost of the relevant contract, except        合約工程的變更、申索及獎金只會在金額
   where this would not be representative of the stage of              能夠可靠地計量及認為可能收取該等金額
   completion. Variations in contract work, claims and incentive       的情況下方會入賬。
   payments are included to the extent that the amount can be
   measured reliably and its receipt is considered probable.

   Where the outcome of a construction contract cannot be              倘工程合約的結果不能可靠地計量，合約
   estimated reliably, contract revenue is recognised to the           收入僅以可能收回的已產生合約成本部分
   extent of contract costs incurred that is probably recoverable.     確認。合約成本於產生期間確認為開支。
   Contract costs are recognised as expenses in the period in
   which they are incurred.

   When it is probable that total contract costs will exceed total     當總合約成本很可能超出總合約收入時，
   contract revenue, the expected loss is recognised as an             則預期虧損將立即確認為開支。
   expense immediately.

   Where contract costs incurred to date plus recognised profits       倘迄今已產生的合約成本加已確認溢利減
   less recognised losses exceed progress billings, the surplus        已確認虧損超過進度款項，有關盈餘將視
   is treated as amounts due from customers for contract work.         為應收客戶的合約工程款項。倘進度款項
   Where progress billings exceed contract costs incurred to           超過迄今已產生合約成本加已確認溢利減
   date plus recognised profits less recognised losses, the            已確認虧損，有關盈餘將視為應付客戶的
   surplus is treated as amounts due to customers for contract         合約工程款項。
   work.

      the temporary investment of specific borrowings pending             本的借貸成本中扣除。所有其他借貸成本

      costs consist of interest and other costs that an entity incurs

      final dividends are disclosed in the notes to the financial

      Performance Obligations                                                履約責任
      Information about the Group’s performance obligations is               有關本集團履約責任的資料概述如下：
      summarised below:

      Sale of goods                                                          銷售貨品
      The performance obligation is satisfied upon delivery of the           本集團於交貨時履行責任，付款一般在交
      goods and payment is generally due within 30 to 360 days               貨後 30 至 360 天內到期，惟新客戶一般須
      from delivery, except for new customers, where payment in              預先付款。
      advance is normally required.

      Renovation, installation and engineering services                      裝修、安裝及工程服務
      The performance obligation is satisfied over time as services          履約責任隨提供服務而隨時間推移履行，
      are rendered and payment is generally due within 30 days               且付款一般於結算日起 30 天內到期。客戶
      from the date of billing. A certain percentage of payment is           保留一定百分比的付款直至保留期結束，
      retained by customers until the end of the retention period as         乃由於本集團獲取最終付款的權利視乎於
      the Group’s entitlement to the final payment is conditional on         客戶按合約規定的一段時間內對服務質素
      the satisfaction of the service quality by the customers over a        表示滿意後方可作實。
      certain period as stipulated in the contracts.

      The transaction prices allocated to the remaining performance          於 2018 年 12 月 31 日，分 配 至 餘 下 履 約 責
      obligations (unsatisfied or partially unsatisfied) as at 31            任（未履約或部分未履約）的交易價格如下：
      December 2018 are as follows:

                                                                                                     RMB’000

       Not more than 1 year                               不多於 1 年                                      356,315
       Over 1 year                                        1 年以上                                        368,211
                                                                                                       724,526

      The remaining performance obligations expected to be                   預期將於一年以上確認的餘下履約責任涉

   The recoverable amounts of GZEP, Huifengyuan, Baolai,             GZEP、匯 豐 源、寶 來、湖 南 華 清、江 淮
   Hunan Huaqing, Jianghuai Inspection and Jiangsu Yongbao           檢測及江蘇永葆的可收回金額乃根據現金
   have been determined based on value in use calculation            流量預測以可使用價值方式計算。該現金

   of credit insurance). The calculation reflects the probability-     現況及未來經濟狀況預測的的合理支持資

   and supportable information that is available at the reporting

   customer type and rating, and coverage by letters of credit or            該計算反映或然率加權的結果、貨幣時間
   other forms of credit insurance). The calculation reflects the            值以及於報告日期可獲得有關過往事件、

   reasonable and supportable information that is available at               料。
   the reporting date about past events, current conditions and

      Pursuant to the PRC Corporate Income Tax Law, a 10%                  根據中國企業所得稅法，在中國內地成立
      withholding tax is levied on dividends declared to foreign           的外資企業向外國投資者宣派的股息須繳
      investors from the foreign investment enterprises established        納 10% 的預提所得稅。該規定自 2008 年 1
      in mainland China. The requirement is effective from 1               月 1 日起生效，適用於自 2007 年 12 月 31 日
      January 2008 and applies to earnings after 31 December               起獲得的盈利。倘中國內地與該外國投資
      2007. A lower withholding tax rate may be applied if there is a      者所在司法權區有簽訂稅務條約，則可按
      tax treaty between mainland China and the jurisdictions of the       較低稅率繳納預提所得稅。本集團的適用
      foreign investors. The applicable rate for the Group is 5%. The      稅率為 5%。因此，就該等於中國內地成立
      Group is therefore liable for withholding taxes on dividends         的 附 屬 公 司 就 2008 年 1 月 1 日 起 所 得 盈 利
      distributed by those subsidiaries established in mainland            而分派的股息而言，本集團須繳納預提所
      China in respect of their earnings generated from 1 January          得稅。
      2008.

      At the end of the reporting period, the Group has not                於 報 告 期 末，由 於 本 公 司 控 制 該 等 附 屬
      recognised deferred tax liabilities of RMB350,709,000 (2017:         公 司 的 股 息 政 策，且 該 等 溢 利 於 可 見 將
      RMB275,791,000) in respect of temporary differences                  來 分 派 的 可 能 性 不 大，故 本 集 團 並 無 確
      relating to the undistributed profits of subsidiaries, amounting     認該等附屬公司未分派溢利暫時差異為
      to RMB7,014,180,000 (2017: RMB5,515,812,000), that would             人 民 幣 7,014,180,000 元（ 2017 年：人
      be payable on the distribution of these retained profits as the      民 幣 5,515,812,000 元）的 遞 延 稅 項 負 債

43. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                 43. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續）
    AND POLICIES (Continued)

      CREDIT RISK                                                          信貸風險
      Credit risk is the risk that a counterparty will not meet its        信貸風險指因交易對手無法履行其於金融
      obligations under a financial instrument or customer contract,       工具或客戶合約項下責任而蒙受財務虧損
      leading to a financial loss. The Group is exposed to credit risk     的風險。本集團所面臨的信貸風險與其貿
      in relation to its trade and bills receivables, contract assets,     易應收款項及票據、合約資產、應收借款、
      loan receivables, other receivables, debt instruments at             其他應收款項、按公允價值計入其他全面
      FVTOCI and cash and bank deposits.                                   收益的債務工具以及現金及銀行存款有關。

      The concession operating right of the Group represent                本集團的特許經營權指有關建造、營運及
      sewage treatment service concession financial receivables            移交安排的特許污水處理服務金融應收款
      in respect of Build-operate-transfer (“BOT”) arrangements.           項。於 報 告 期 末，鑑 於 建 造、營 運 及 移 交
      Since the parties to BOT arrangements are local government          （「建造、營運及移交」）安排的訂約方均為
      authorities in the PRC, the Group considers the credit risk is       中國地方政府機關，故本集團認為信貸風
      low at the end of reporting period. The Group does not hold          險偏低。本集團並無就有關結餘持有任何
      any collateral over these balances.                                  抵押品。

      In relation to the Group’s financial services business,              有關本集團的金融服務業務，本集團擁有
      the Group has a credit team which is responsible for the             信貸團隊負責評估客戶的信貸評級、財務
      evaluation of customers’ credit ratings, financial background        背景及償還能力。本集團僅向信譽良好的
      and repayment abilities. The Group only provides credit to           客戶提供信貸及一般須提供抵押品。該等
      customers with creditworthiness and collateral is normally           客戶的付款拖欠風險並不重大。
      required. The risk of default in payment by these customers is
      considered not significant.

      The Group has policies in place to ensure that sales are made        本集團訂有政策確保向擁有穩健財政實力
      to purchasers with an appropriate ﬁnancial strength and              及可支付適當百分比首期付款的買方作出
      appropriate percentage of down payments. It also has other           銷售，亦設有其他監控程序以確保採取跟
      monitoring procedures to ensure that follow-up action is taken       進 行 動 收 回 逾 期 債 務。此 外，本 集 團 定 期
      to recover overdue debts. In addition, the Group reviews             審閱各項個別貿易應收款項及票據和合約
      regularly the recoverable amount of each individual trade and        資產的可收回金額，以確保就不可收回金
      bills receivables and contract assets to ensure that adequate        額作出足夠的減值虧損。本集團的信貸風
      impairment losses are made for irrecoverable amounts. The            險源自多名交易對手及客戶，故並無重大
      Group has no signiﬁcant concentrations of credit risk, with          信貸集中風險。
      exposure spread over a large number of counterparties and
      customers.

      For other receivables, an impairment analysis is performed           就其他應收款項而言，管理層透過考慮歷
      by management at the end of each reporting period by                 史結算記錄、過往經驗及將來經濟狀況於
      considering historical settlement records, past experience           各報告期末進行減值分析。本公司董事認
      and future economic conditions. The directors of the                 為，本集團其他應收款項的未償還結餘並
      Company believe that there is no material credit risk inherent       無重大固有信貸風險。
      in the Group’s outstanding balance of other receivables.

   The Group’s debt instruments at FVTOCI comprised solely                               本集團按公允價值計入其他全面收益的債

   investments.

   Bank deposits are mainly placed with state-owned ﬁnancial                             銀行存款主要存放於國有金融機構及信譽

   quality ﬁnancial institutions.                                                        構）。

   The Group considers the probability of default upon initial                           本集團於初始確認金融資產時考慮違約或
   recognition of financial assets and whether there has been                            然率，並考慮各報告期內的信貸風險有否
   a signiﬁcant increase in credit risk on an ongoing basis                              持續顯著增加。為評估信貸風險是否顯著
   throughout each reporting period. To assess whether there                             增加，本集團將於報告日期資產發生違約
   is a signiﬁcant increase in credit risk the Group compares the                        的風險與於初始確認日期的違約風險進行
   risk of a default occurring on the asset as at the reporting date                     比較。本集團考慮可獲得的合理支持性前
   with the risk of default as at the date of initial recognition. It                    瞻資料。
   considers available reasonable and supportive forward-looking
   information.

   CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                    資本管理
   The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are          本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本集團
   to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as going concern         繼續以持續基準經營的能力，以及維持合
   and to maintain reasonable capital ratios in order to support its     理的資本比率支持其業務，以及將股東的
   business and maximise shareholders’ value.                            價值提升至最高。

   The Group manages its capital structure and makes                     本集團根據經濟狀況變動管理其資本架構
   adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions.         及作出調整。為優化債務及權益結餘，本集
   In order to optimise the debt and equity balance, the Group           團可調整支付予股東的股息、向股東退回
   may adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return               資 本、發 行 新 股 或 新 債 或 贖 回 現 有 債 務。
   capital to shareholders, issue new shares or new debts or the         年 內，管 理 資 本 的 目 標、政 策 或 過 程 並 無
   redemption of existing debts. No changes were made in the             變動。
   objectives, policies or processes for managing capital during
   the year.

   The Group monitors capital using a net debt to equity ratio,          本集團以債務淨額對權益比率監控資本，
   which is net debt divided by capital. Net debt includes               該比率為債務淨額除以資本。債務淨額包
   borrowings less cash and bank deposits. Capital represents            括借款減現金及銀行存款。資本乃指權益
   the total equity.                                                     總額。

   The Group’s strategy is to maintain the net debt to equity            本集團的策略為維持債務淨額對權益比率
   ratio at a healthy level in order to support its businesses. The      在 健 康 水 平，以 支 持 其 業 務。本 集 團 採 取
   principal strategies adopted by the Group include, without            的主要策略包括但不限於審閱未來現金流
   limitation, reviewing future cash flow requirements and               量需求及支付到期債務的能力，保持可用
   the ability to meet debt repayment schedules when they                銀行融資在合理水平及調整投資計劃及融
   fall due, maintaining a reasonable level of available banking         資計劃（如需要），以確保本集團擁有合理
   facilities and adjusting investment plans and financing plans, if     水平的資本支持其業務。於報告期末的債
   necessary, to ensure that the Group has a reasonable level of         務淨額對權益比率如下：
   capital to support its business. The net debt to equity ratios,
